    "e1791c7d0e2f79b5cbfec67321c1ce9116c4e2140faa1578998105f04abc7a38": "# Private fixture authority\nThe local batch-import direction and D0/C0 have separately attributable fictional settled assessments on exactly the baseline subjects. Plan is a draft. No permission to execute commands, initialize work, publish or advance to another stage follows merely from these facts. Each case may explicitly supply one narrower action authorization. Independently authorized neighbor wording correction has complete separate inputs and remains permitted when unrelated allocation is blocked. Command labels row-check, old-batch-check and current-batch-check are opaque fixture labels; only the last two are explicitly equivalent for the same complete destination-preservation observation. They are not repository commands and must not be executed.\n\nThe stable Plan handoff pointer docs/changes/batch-import/change.json is a synthetic owner label in this packet, not a created or validated runtime record. The corrected baseline has a separately stipulated fictional assessment of its exact new D0/C0 subjects. Copied history/R0.txt belongs to the original packet generation: preserve its bytes and original subject identities; do not reuse or retarget that historical judgment as approval of this corrected baseline. Actual packet provenance binds both generations in the manifest.\n",
    "ec195039ce39bd56557ced54b6c1a2a3bea4e5bcef9965bca894be48cf20d115": "# Local batch import\n\n## Challenge\nManual row entry makes a valid batch cumbersome and a rejected batch must not leave a partially updated destination.\n\n## Goals\nCommit every valid row; if any row is invalid, retain exactly the original destination. Make rejection visible to the user.\n\n## Scope and non-goals\nLocal batch import only. No remote publication, automatic next-stage execution, network service or change to existing single-row behavior. A runtime storage outage and concurrent writers require their own specified behavior before any broader guarantee is claimed; this fixture selects validation rejection.\n\n## Governing principle\nA rejected batch must leave the user where they started.\n\n## Proposed direction\nValidate a complete batch before crossing the persistence boundary. Preserve the all-or-nothing outcome for invalid input; Design owns realization.\n\n## Feasibility\nThe supplied storage interface supports one validated batch write. This is a bounded fixture assumption, not live provider evidence. If that assumption fails, return the constraint and options to the direction owner without weakening the goal.\n\n## Decision requested\nApprove this direction for independently reviewed Design work only. This draft grants no Design approval, implementation, publication or merge authority.\n",
    "e13b5a9c683d734b85dc380b353a77fbff823d48ea67b5961ec31ed39a8ac457": "# Batch import delivery plan\n\n## Purpose / big picture\n\nDeliver the approved local batch-import behavior without weakening invalid-batch destination preservation. This is stable synthetic allocation; no command, current review result or milestone completion is claimed.\n\n## Current Handoff Summary\n\n- Owning change record: docs/changes/batch-import/change.json (stable synthetic pointer supplied by authority.md; this packet contains no runtime record and claims no record-schema conformance)\n\nMutable lifecycle state, current milestone state, review status, blockers, routing, and closeout readiness live only in this record.\n\n## Source artifacts\n\n- Proposal: [Direction](proposal.md)\n- Spec: [Design D0](importer/importer.md); living model, no legacy feature output\n- Architecture: [Importer architecture](importer/importer.md#architecture-overview)\n- Prior-contract test spec: none; model-owned [C0 coverage](importer/test-design/cases.md) supplies intent, with no companion test-spec\n\n## Context and orientation\n\n[Design D0](importer/importer.md) and [coverage C0](importer/test-design/cases.md) own the behavior and observations. Importer combines Input validation and Store persistence; [(9, retained)] is the original private destination. D0/C0 assessments and command availability are expressly fictional supplied facts. [Authority](authority.md) bounds actions; history and neighbor.txt remain preserved.\n\n## Non-goals\n\n- No network, remote publication, actual command execution, automatic continuation, storage-outage/concurrency guarantee or change to single-row behavior.\n\n## Requirements covered\n\n- IMPORT-SR-01: M1 and TG-1 input decisions.\n- IMPORT-SR-02: M2 and TG-2 actual success/rejection destination observations; TG-FINAL-1 checks their composed path.\n- R-old: preserve the existing unusual-valid-value regression; catalog absence alone does not permit removal.\n\n## Milestones\n\n### M1. Input validation\n\n- Milestone kind: implementation\n- Engineering purpose: Establish the complete input decision before any persistence connection.\n- Requirements: IMPORT-SR-01\n- Architecture responsibility: Input child; importer owns later invocation order under IMPORT-DEC-01.\n- Dependencies:\n  - Current proposal and exact D0/C0 have the separately stipulated independent assessments; this allocation grants no implementation permission.\n- Implementation scope: Implement row and duplicate-ID checks only; retain existing single-row behavior and introduce no persistence writes.\n- Files/components likely touched:\n  - Input validation component and local partition tests.\n- Required verification:\n  - TG-1 — Integer IDs, nonempty values and duplicate IDs select the intended acceptance/rejection; valid rows remain accepted.\n- Evidence expectations: Literal valid/invalid rows, intended diagnostics, exact subject identities and actual outcomes when executed.\n- Implementation steps:\n  - Establish the valid three-row control, introduce each defining invalid condition in a fresh input, prove expected failure when feasible, implement the rule and rerun the same proof.\n- Validation commands:\n  - row-check (stipulated project-supported command label; no invocation is performed by this packet).\n- Expected observable result: All three valid rows pass; each malformed row or duplicate-ID batch produces its intended rejection.\n- Completion criteria: Every allocated input partition has independent expected observations and current passing proof; no intended behavior remains unresolved.\n- Required evidence: Input/source/test identities, actual row-check observations and limitations in implementation-owned evidence.\n- Review handoff: Independent milestone Code Review of the Input change and its concrete assertions before dependent connection work.\n- Optional commit boundary: `M1: Input validation`\n- Risks:\n  - A broken input fixture can masquerade as rejection protection.\n- Rollback/recovery:\n  - Restore the local Input slice if a valid control or protected partition fails; preserve unrelated user data and do not begin M2.\n\n### M2. Batch write boundary\n\n- Milestone kind: implementation\n- Engineering purpose: Connect complete validation to persistence as one reviewable change with its allocated proof.\n- Requirements: IMPORT-SR-02; preserve IMPORT-SR-01 and R-old.\n- Architecture responsibility: Importer orchestration of Input and Store, IMPORT-DEC-01, with parent-owned validation-to-write observation.\n- Dependencies:\n  - M1's current independent milestone review and complete input behavior.\n- Implementation scope: Connect the complete validator to one batch Store call; preserve single-row behavior. Outage/concurrency guarantees remain outside this fixture.\n- Files/components likely touched:\n  - Importer batch connection and its allocated test fixtures.\n- Required verification:\n  - TG-2 — Prove both IMPORT-TC-01/02 through the real public boundary and private destination with independent before/after rows.\n- Evidence expectations: Exact subjects, inputs, intended diagnostics and independent complete destination snapshots for success and rejection; preserve the unusual-valid-value R-old regression.\n- Implementation steps:\n  - First prove both IMPORT-TC-01/02 through the real public boundary and private destination with independent before/after rows. Then implement the connection, rerun required proof and retain the independent expected records.\n- Validation commands:\n  - old-batch-check (stipulated command label; no invocation is performed by this packet).\n- Expected observable result: Valid input leaves exactly [(9, retained), (1, alpha), (2, beta), (3, gamma)]; invalid middle value reports that rejection and leaves exactly [(9, retained)].\n- Completion criteria: Current allocated observations pass and independent milestone review is complete; an unresolved required observation blocks completion.\n- Required evidence: Implementation-owned exact subjects, command, complete destination observations and limitations; no authored allocation is a passing run.\n- Review handoff: Independent Code Review of the complete batch connection and allocated assertions; preserve distinct final whole-change review.\n- Optional commit boundary: `M2: Batch write boundary`\n- Risks:\n  - A per-row write loop can commit row1 before rejecting row2; valid-only observations would miss it.\n- Rollback/recovery:\n  - Revert the connection and its fixtures together if required protection fails, preserving M1, prior records and unrelated work.\n\n\n## Final review checkpoint\n\n- Kind: lifecycle-closeout.\n- Dependency: all in-scope implementation milestones and required corrections complete.\n- Assessment: fresh independent final whole-change Code Review of the complete delivered engineering change and cross-milestone interactions.\n- Evidence: exact final subjects, independent reviewer basis, judgment and concern dispositions.\n- Successor: final Verify; corrections return to their owner and require affected reassessment.\n\nThis checkpoint applies the selected review policy. A verification-group non-applicability rationale does not waive it.\n\n## Change-level verification\n\n### TG-FINAL-1. Preserve complete batch behavior across Input and Store\n\n- Covers: IMPORT-SR-01/02, M1/M2, public import-to-Store path and retained R-old protection.\n- Demonstrate: Invoke the same public boundary on valid rows and a fresh invalid-middle-row variation. Compare the entire destination to independently specified rows; an implementation that leaves (1, alpha) after rejection must fail. Preserve the unusual valid value and expose actual diagnostics.\n- Evidence expectations: Actual project-supported batch proof with private real persistence, exact subjects/commands/inputs/results and independent Code Review; this packet supplies no execution results.\n- Non-applicability: Not claimed: the composed public path remains required even if local child checks pass. Required final whole-change Code Review is a separate checkpoint.\n\n## Validation plan\n\n- row-check: input partitions and local rejection meaning.\n- old-batch-check: IMPORT-TC-01/02 on the real public/private-destination boundary with complete independent before/after rows, plus R-old.\n- Independent artifact/implementation review: inspect scope, exact subjects, sequencing and whether actual assertions detect partial persistence; no phrase check substitutes for judgment.\n- Command labels are stipulated fixture inputs, not repository commands to execute; confirm actual project support before implementation.\n\n## Risks and recovery\n\n- Risk: Per-row writes or a parser-only replacement can hide partial persistence. Mutable results embedded in this plan would create false authority.\n  - Recovery: Revert the affected connection and its fixtures together, preserve M1 and unrelated user records, and return missing behavior/allocation to the responsible owner. Keep observed results outside stable intent.\n\n## Dependencies\n\n- Current exact proposal/Design authority precedes Plan reliance; M1 proof/review precedes M2; all implementation/corrections precede fresh whole-change Code Review and distinct final Verify. No external action follows from this allocation.\n\n## Decision log\n\n| Date | Decision | Reason | Alternatives rejected |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| 2026-09-17 | Validate the complete batch before connecting one Store write; retain real destination proof after local input proof. | The approved invalid-input guarantee prohibits partial persistence, while dependency order keeps intermediate work reviewable. | Per-row validate/write can retain row1 after row2 fails; parser-only proof cannot detect that state. |\n\n\n## Readiness\n\n- See the owning change record for current workflow state.\n",
    "9e5200268ffd648c4014cba724642fb5996a7906da0730545b656f1f9b3ce802": "Fictional independent reviewer R assessed exact original D0/C0; original subject hashes are in baseline manifest. This is immutable fixture history, not a real approval or execution authorization.\n",
    "6ab2f34ba602596d88b2a24dfcdd8bcfa772bba65720d5dcf09c5891ce8bde38": "# Importer Design\n\nModel validation contract: model-document-v1\n\n## Introduction and goals\nOwner importer combines child Input validation and child Store persistence. Preserve the approved invalid-batch no-write outcome, with one current owner for each child rule and this composed guarantee.\n\n## Architecture constraints\nDirection is the exact approved proposal; no remote publication is authorized. Input supplies validated rows; Store accepts the complete validated batch. Installation, public release and outage/concurrency guarantees are outside this bounded fixture.\n\n## Architecture overview\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n  User[\"Local caller\"] -->|batch request| Importer\n  subgraph ImporterModel[\"Importer model\"]\n    Importer[\"Batch orchestration\"] -->|validate complete batch| Input[\"Input child: row and duplicate rules\"]\n    Input -->|validated batch or rejection| Importer\n    Importer -->|only validated batch| Store\n    Store[\"Store child: validated batch persistence\"]\n  end\n  Store -->|apply validated rows| Destination[\"Existing local destination\"]\n  Importer -->|complete success or validation rejection| User\n```\nThe model owns the orchestration and its two child responsibilities; the caller and destination are external interfaces. Detailed owners are the [context](#context-view), [building blocks](#building-block-view) and [runtime](#runtime-and-interfaces), rather than this summary alone.\n\n| View | Necessity and reason | Owning detail |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Context | Necessary: local caller, accepted direction and destination have distinct external roles. | [Context view](#context-view) |\n| Building Block | Necessary: orchestration, input validation and persistence have different duties and their composition can fail. | [Building Block view](#building-block-view) |\n| Runtime | Necessary: rejection must precede any write. | [Runtime and interfaces](#runtime-and-interfaces) |\n| Deployment | Not necessary: one local process/private destination; no separately deployed service or environment-specific guarantee is selected. | Architecture constraints; adding concurrency or outage behavior requires reassessment. |\n\n## Context view\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n  Direction[\"Approved direction owner\"] -.->|invalid input must preserve prior records| ImporterSystem[\"Importer\"]\n  Caller[\"Local caller\"] -->|complete input batch| ImporterSystem\n  ImporterSystem -->|success or specific validation rejection| Caller\n  ImporterSystem -->|validated batch updates only| Destination[\"Existing local destination\"]\n```\nThe caller submits data and receives the actual result; it does not bypass validation. The direction owner defines the supported invalid-input guarantee and remains the recipient of material infeasibility. The local destination begins with independent prior records and may change only after complete validation. Network publication and outage/concurrency semantics are outside the selected contract.\n\n## Building Block view\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n  Batch[\"Public batch interface\"] -->|entire request| Orchestrator[\"Importer orchestration\"]\n  Orchestrator -->|all rows and IDs| Validator[\"Input child\"]\n  Validator -->|validated whole batch or defined rejection| Orchestrator\n  Orchestrator -->|one complete validated batch after success| Writer[\"Store child\"]\n  Writer -->|apply rows| LocalRecords[\"Local destination\"]\n  Orchestrator -->|actual completion or rejection| Result[\"Caller result\"]\n```\nInput owns integer-ID, nonempty-value and within-batch duplicate checks under IMPORT-SR-01. Store owns the stipulated local operation accepting one complete validated batch; it does not decide input acceptance or authorize partial imports. Importer owns the invocation order and caller result under IMPORT-SR-02 and IMPORT-DEC-01. Neither child's isolated proof establishes that the public importer waits for complete validation; that interaction needs the real destination observations in C0.\n\n## Requirements\n| ID | Required behavior |\n| --- | --- |\n| IMPORT-SR-01 | Each row has an integer ID and nonempty value; duplicate IDs within the batch reject. |\n| IMPORT-SR-02 | An invalid batch leaves the destination unchanged; a valid batch commits every row. |\n\n### Boundary scan and acceptance scenarios\n| Dimension | Requirement basis | Distinct outcome to demonstrate |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Input domain | IMPORT-SR-01 | Integer/nonempty/duplicate distinctions yield exact acceptance or rejection. |\n| State/lifecycle | IMPORT-SR-02 | Invalid input preserves original rows; valid batch adds all rows. |\n| Identity/authority | - | Not applicable: local input has no principal/authorization rule in this bounded model. |\n| Composition/path | IMPORT-SR-02 | Full validation precedes Store invocation; per-row validation alone cannot prove this. |\n| Temporal/retry | - | Not applicable: retries and concurrent writers are explicitly outside this fixture's supported scope. |\n| Failure/recovery | IMPORT-SR-01, IMPORT-SR-02 | Invalid middle row gives nonzero rejection and no destination mutation. |\n| Compatibility/migration | - | Not applicable: no persisted-format migration or retired operation is introduced. |\n| External/environment | IMPORT-SR-02 | Observe the real local destination; no network dependency is claimed. |\n\n## Runtime and interfaces\nThe importer validates all rows and duplicate IDs before invoking Store once. If the middle row is invalid, it returns a validation failure without invoking Store. The valid batch write adds all three rows to the existing destination. Children do not own the parent's sequencing guarantee.\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n  User->>Importer: batch\n  Importer->>Input: all rows\n  alt invalid row\n    Input-->>Importer: rejection\n    Importer-->>User: nonzero validation failure\n  else all valid\n    Input-->>Importer: validated complete batch\n    Importer->>Store: write complete batch\n    Importer-->>User: complete success\n  end\n```\n\n## Test design\nSee [owned coverage](test-design/cases.md). IMPORT-TC-01/02 retain conditions, independent observations and proposed realization here, independently of a Delivery plan. Parent owns the Input-to-Store no-write interaction; per-row checks cannot observe it.\n\n## Decisions and risk\nIMPORT-DEC-01 chooses complete validation before write over a validate/write loop: the latter can leave row1 behind after row2 fails. Preserve that alternative and consequence. Unknown protected behavior returns to Design; material infeasibility returns to the direction owner.\n\n## Supporting examples\n[Example E0](examples/rejection.md) is an explicitly declared illustration of IMPORT-SR-02, not a workflow record or executed test.\n\n## Limits and handoff\nExact model, coverage and relied-on example go to independent Design Review. Proposed tests are unexecuted. No authored document or save receipt supplies approval.\n",
    "f2d744cd3b7631c1e119855cb87f6d7544f68364fb5f40a5bfe75a349632dbd9": "# Importer coverage C0\nOwner importer. Requirements IMPORT-SR-01 and IMPORT-SR-02. Proposed realization only; no executed result.\n\nIMPORT-TC-01: Fresh private destination contains exactly [(9, retained)]. Import [(1, alpha), (2, beta), (3, gamma)] through the public batch interface. Observe complete success and exactly [(9, retained), (1, alpha), (2, beta), (3, gamma)]. Literal expected rows are independent of the importer.\n\nIMPORT-TC-02: Establish the valid setup above, then use a fresh destination with the same original row and change only the middle value to empty. Invoke the same public boundary. Observe the specific empty-value rejection, nonzero status, and complete destination exactly [(9, retained)]. Keep real persistence and compare the whole destination; parser-only checks miss a partial row1 write. Each case owns mutable state and cleanup.\n\nR-old: A supported unusual but valid value such as 'alpha beta' remains protected by an existing regression even though it has no separate scenario row. Preserve until independent assessment establishes equivalent detection or the behavior owner authorizes retirement.\n",
    "58bbb95dc5f54a49ddce6a0ab4968516edd7ef57539b5d57bbdf0760f92e02fb": "# Rejection example E0\n\nDeclared excerpt, not a complete record. Before: [(9, retained)]. Input: [(1, alpha), (2, empty), (3, gamma)]. After: [(9, retained)]. Neighbor metadata remains owner=local.\n",
    "5d18a8f822295d549af39ee0ea684eae4a720f08193237dc16ee1af32d0315e0": "# Fictional customer-project authority\n\nOwner: Project Owner O. This packet explicitly adopts current RigorLoop Review and Closeout and shared test criteria for the named atomic-import change. Exact current subjects and required independent review govern reliance; installing skills alone adopts nothing. User authority is the exact request in facts.md. Review actors R and V did not author the target contribution by A; repairer C is a different person from R and V. A may restrict applicability but cannot renew R or close V's blocker. Route owns workflow decisions; CLI save is persistence only. No external mutation, release or real repository execution is authorized.\n\nThe full M4 files in this project are read-only reusable fixture inputs. Their historical authority.md and R0.txt retain original meaning; this Constitution and context.json stipulate the separately current M6 subject basis, without retargeting R0. Labels and supplied observations are fictional semantic inputs, not observations of a target agent or an executed validation command. M6 candidate actions/results are proposed artifacts for independent assessment.\n",
    "b64fd1ab9eb77b30a1f4791534cacd0e65c5e0e517f02041f615f302cb5b5ba1": "def import_rows(rows, destination):\n    parsed = []\n    seen = set()\n    for item in rows:\n        identifier, value = item\n        if not isinstance(identifier, int) or not isinstance(value, str) or not value or identifier in seen:\n            raise ValueError(\"invalid row\")\n        seen.add(identifier)\n        parsed.append((identifier, value))\n    destination.extend(parsed)\n    return len(parsed)\n",
    "95ec9f16e1c2308b7ed58871d7a0b76f288c3d66b1110f42dad1d6ceb17a951b": "import unittest\nfrom src.importer import import_rows\n\nclass ImportTests(unittest.TestCase):\n    def test_valid_batch(self):\n        destination = [(9, \"retained\")]\n        self.assertEqual(import_rows([(1,\"alpha\"),(2,\"beta\"),(3,\"gamma\")], destination), 3)\n        self.assertEqual(destination, [(9,\"retained\"),(1,\"alpha\"),(2,\"beta\"),(3,\"gamma\")])\n    def test_invalid_middle_preserves_destination(self):\n        destination = [(9, \"retained\")]\n        before = list(destination)\n        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, \"invalid row\"):\n            import_rows([(1,\"alpha\"),(2,\"\"),(3,\"gamma\")], destination)\n        self.assertEqual(destination, before)\n    def test_unlisted_space_value_regression(self):\n        destination = [(9,\"retained\")]\n        self.assertEqual(import_rows([(1,\"alpha beta\")], destination), 1)\n        self.assertEqual(destination, [(9,\"retained\"),(1,\"alpha beta\")])\n    def test_row_domain_rejects_without_mutation(self):\n        for rows in [[(\"one\",\"alpha\")],[(1,\"\")],[(1,\"alpha\"),(1,\"beta\")]]:\n            with self.subTest(rows=rows):\n                destination = [(9,\"retained\")]\n                before = list(destination)\n                with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,\"invalid row\"):\n                    import_rows(rows, destination)\n                self.assertEqual(destination, before)\n",

    "decfd60bf198106a0befe6555d0cf8683a341a205b13b42e4001e1f70d79fce2": "# M6 historical reliance preparation\n\nRead-only preparation, 2026-09-18. Scope: SKL-SR-16–23/30–33, WF-SR-10/18, RC-SR-16/21 and HAND-SR-04. No repository edits, case creation, records, tests, routing or approvals. File reads and Git-object reads below establish what the historical records say and which bytes they identify; they are not renewed semantic judgments. M6 implementation still awaits its preceding gate.\n\n## Original proposal/proposal-review pilot: SKL-SR-16–23\n\nOwning change: `docs/changes/2026-09-08-skill-model-proposal-family-pilot/`. Actual implementation commit `b8f87a4c1216c41ce711e22a62d5b21b8c3ab0f8`; final engineering correction `24c92999e8cd16d61e942a6b6a9f5100a1e064ab`. Original M1 source/artifact walkthrough is recoverable at the former commit's `m1-procedure-walkthrough.md`; its raw SHA-256 is `0137b1cc06e8e59f722ac53e7cebe23ff30c8d1cf0ca94575d80efbbad6bc5f9`, independently recomputed in this read-only preparation. Current `evidence.json#preserved-m1-procedure-walkthrough` preserves that exact narrative with an explicit storage-only wrapper. Commit `1acf15f0fa13839fb68bea27633906313e87e2ff` moved narratives into registered evidence to resolve `skl-cr-001`; no old approval was renewed by the move.\n\nThe walkthrough concretely compares recording-first bodies with classification-first bodies and complete existing conditional references. It contains a full seven-section proposal and an advisory output specimen, plus portable/governed/formal/late-trigger, adopted-policy, unavailable-method, conflict/recovery and uncertain-target observations. Its governed example explicitly used the then-current v2 store. It expressly claims source/artifact inspection, **not** agent execution or a runtime benchmark. `code-review-m1-r1` independently approved M1; final `code-review-final-r2` and distinct successful Verify close the exact later corrected package. Current M4/M6 proof must use current v3, not alter the old specimen or pretend its v2 path is operational now.\n\n`evidence.json#preserved-m2-implementation` preserves source transfer (original narrative SHA-256 `b259e596cd9b21a81815fd238ff7a2c4c112fb3b2c48fc15573dfef5383a3a27`): four original snapshots, 73 resolving archive links, mixed Skill Contract R37–45/R56–63 retained at that time, common architecture flow 1–7 transferred while 8–10 retained, explicit governance/validator/selector/package consumer dispositions, and original-byte assertions. It identifies one already-unresolved illustrative `plans/YYYY-MM-DD-slug.md` target rather than inventing it. The snapshots' former active/archive paths are now subject to later complete source retirement; this old allocation does not require restoring them.\n\n**Current reliance:** SKL-SR-16–18/20 still protect the selected pair's current selection, complete procedure, outputs and readers. SKL-SR-19 is the accepted original improvement obligation, with inspection-based evidence and its limits; no recurring token benchmark is selected. SKL-SR-21/22's source-transfer/adoption episode has recorded original evidence, independent reviews and Verify; subsequent source changes require their own authority and consumer reconciliation. SKL-SR-23's remaining families were explicitly allocated, not completed by this pair.\n\n## Later inventory simplification: SKL-SR-30–32\n\nOwning change: `docs/changes/2026-09-15-skill-simplification/`. Original pair baseline and advisory bytes are recoverable at `6ffd08e9cce0d3ab28090fab3b1570adbd50a84f`; `evidence.json#m1-advisory-provenance` identifies the exact advisory path. Whole-change base is `51833994a520bf29c9aae135e9405331cf76b755`; final engineering is `de0e6cec98a2f485b04cf3fd09f08db8fb389f34`. `m1-obligations` compares complete implement/code-review packages, independent IP/recording classification, unchanged shared sections, readers and ordinary/exceptional paths. It records observed navigation/coherence improvement, not merely smaller line counts; its line counts are explicitly orientation only.\n\n`m2-obligations` through `m6-obligations` and `integrated-inventory` allocate every one of nineteen capabilities: changed implement/code-review, plan/proposal-review, ci-maintenance, constitution, explore/learn; retained proposal/design/design-review/delivery-review, route/verify/pr, bugfix, vision/project-map, research. Thus eight changed plus eleven retained is an actual attributed inventory, not inferred from the first pilot. `integrated-inventory` itself was pre-M6-review and must be read with final `final-code-review-rereview`, subsequent final candidate/full-range proof, and successful Verify. Final review records all 19/140 canonical files and the whole handoff chain; Verify binds the complete range through `9e586773d1008ec8c0ee20ccbc98494b8c7c0d8a` and separately disclaims runtime-agent execution, measured usability/token savings and hosted CI.\n\n**Current reliance:** the scoped all-19 simplification has final independent closure evidence, while SKL-SR-31's implement/code-review selection and procedure obligations continue. SKL-SR-32 requires complete affected reading-path assessment when simplification changes those paths; it does not re-open the finished historical initiative on every later test change or impose a token/length gate.\n\n## Complete refinement: SKL-SR-33 and current affected packages\n\nOwning change: `docs/changes/2026-09-15-refine-skills-and-retire-stale-support/`; base `3c4985f80b242a295116880a5aee9cbbf47b12b0`, frozen final review input `831831935794a9dc30f09aa50b474efcd2e0a02a`, final review commit `20d1ecd170d6031f9081fbc4a015dd5194e28609`, final Verify commit `d72c842b50cdcc2973de21608abde15d2652a1c9`.\n\n`evidence.json#inventory-reconciliation` contains a per-package 19-row complete-package disposition, an independently authored preparation inventory, exact shared-copy populations, and the allocated 111-member source/test/support reconciliation. The preparation portion explicitly was not frozen Code Review; rely on the separate final Code Review for the exact final range. Final review covers all 19 packages and full support populations, selected five recording-relocation paths, current descriptions/output aids, caller composition and retired support. Successful Verify binds these together and preserves the important limitation: generated-only adapter-support migration remained outside this maintenance scope, so live `dist/adapters` readers were retained. That unresolved Packaging-owned debt is not silently closed by either refinement or M6 test work.\n\n**Current reliance:** SKL-SR-33's specified refinement has its own all-package evidence and final judgments, independently of the earlier pilot. Current M6 semantic packets and native reader checks demonstrate present selected contracts; they do not claim to repeat 19-package improvement or renew prior review. New contradictory residue, changed packages or overlooked required proof still goes to its current owner.\n\n## Open remaining adoption is real: SKL-SR-23/30\n\nCurrent `docs/follow-ups.md` FU-015–018 are all **open**. Each names the repository maintainer as accountable and assigns receiving capability owners: authoring/foundations; independent reviewers; coordination/execution/recovery; discovery/learning. Each now links the bounded simplification evidence while explicitly reserving further model/layout adoption to a separate owner decision. This is compatible with the closed bounded 19-package simplification and complete refinement. Neither those completed changes nor present structural tests justify marking the broader follow-ups done. Current Skill's capability adoption boundary says the same. Preserve these IDs/statuses; M6 requires no unrequested adoption work or new backlog copy.\n\n## Original policy transfer and v2 retirement: RC-SR-16/21; WF-SR-10/18\n\nPolicy extraction is `docs/changes/2026-09-07-unify-review-closeout-policy/`: initial implementation `078af3756d5d21147e8a7a69455c382fb3e9ef81`; final public-portability correction engineering `baa1da618337c9606fc24231d56ecaece529554f`; renewed final review and distinct Verify recorded at `66767d3b0e8662aeecf315b7992df25e914ce168`. Final review covers the complete `873f0a238ac5d6d240d5920946a882b54f0e4175..baa1da...` range, four assessment/ten reliance consumers and preserved specialist methods, and expressly limits semantic evidence to manual source-guidance assessment. The original Verify selected `rigorloop-records-v2` and retains that meaning. Do not convert it to a current operational assessment or copy its approval onto changed subjects.\n\nOriginal clause-level owner and consumer mappings remain retrievable at `38a3042e63c7c2462ecf8ffed29f4ac0cbb8923f:docs/design/skill/assessment.md` (“Source transfers and consumer obligations” and “Historical applicability and adoption”), and the equivalent Skill/Workflow files at that commit. Current owners explicitly designate these as provenance, not runtime prerequisites. Later complete source cleanup has its own `2026-09-14-retire-specs-and-stale-tests/source-disposition.md` and source inventory, based on original sources at `7ad33e1b1827c84dfa4e9fbbfc8b52a204b5139e`; it supersedes instructions to retain now-retired source directories, not the substantive surviving obligations.\n\nActual v2 retirement is `docs/changes/2026-09-11-retire-v2-record-format/`. `evidence.json#tg00-pre-removal-disposition` records real local header enumeration, complete selected CLI reads for 12 exact stores, referenced final proof/judgments and Verify limitations, lock/journal inspection and immediate unchanged-revision recheck. This is distinct from the earlier user/owner-confirmed completed-work baseline: the latter must never be called an executed inventory. `material-decisions.json#v2-operational-dependency-disposition`, actor `codex-route`, separately dispositions those twelve exact local stores under approved TG-00/WF-SR-18. Its subjects include original policy Verify `e7c155...` and pilot Verify `1ada63...` (full identities below). This is an actual accountable disposition, not a completion-label inference.\n\nM1 evidence retargets the existing 42-case shared persistence family to synthetic v3 and retains the existing 13 v3 cases, preserving safety without making a new retirement suite. M2 removes exclusive v2 support and reconciles consumers at `66a10cf1a9b6a0ea8e98116a2bc3f20ecf555801`; final engineering later includes consolidated Design through `ff9154a661183a88ee0afbb17330761b2c6d4c58`. The final-consolidation Code Review explicitly marks the earlier final review historical/stale. Use its approval and the distinct final Verify at `5861c2b1671f594e9bd4c449754914d460cd43fd` for that original refined scope, not the superseded earlier final-review judgment.\n\n**Current reliance:** current v3 recording/recovery and historical no-mutation/no-approval-inheritance remain recurring obligations. The twelve-store inspection and 4,511-file historical byte-inventory claims are original local observations, not current or global coverage. No new v2 runtime, migration, retirement/rejection/archive-compatibility suite or reenactment is needed. Current contrary operational/governance residue requires an explicit bounded owner disposition; unknown external stores were expressly excluded from the original closure.\n\n## HAND-SR-04 presentation history and current rationale\n\nThe earlier `2026-08-31-simplify-final-verification-retire-explain-change` change provides archived transition history: final review `reviews/code-review-final-r1.md` was `clean-with-notes` on complete M1–M5 candidate `c93e38340170c9c0e336bb6e3e253469ec4380ac`, expressly excluding historical-v2 closeout. Its later `verify-report.md`, recorded at `d5a1d7b7ea25fe6e3772b391eb62e056fe235ba2`, binds corrected product `9c364d6162a32a03ac63d81093e728fd0e41b0bc` and historical explanation handoff `f3f1f440205e61be8da525b1fd7fe7e174db2604`. Those version-bound transition procedures are historical; do not require today's PR to recreate them or rewrite old bodies.\n\nCurrent HAND-SR-04's exact presentation correction is also covered by the later complete-refinement evidence/final review/Verify above: PR body cites successful Verify rationale and living Design context, without separate Explain-change or invented ADR. Present M6 must inspect a complete current body/resource control and contrary candidate. The history supplies provenance only; successful Verify/current exact-head readiness and external mutation authority remain separate current prerequisites.\n\n## Evidence limits and responsible gaps\n\nThis preparation found concrete recorded historical closure, not missing historical closure hidden by completed labels. It has not replayed historical commands, independently re-adjudicated every clause, authenticated actor identities cryptographically, inspected unknown external stores or established present runtime-agent accuracy. Exact records retain their own evidence/subject arrays and limitations for any necessary deeper review. Original held-out/conditional paths must not become current runtime requirements merely because they appear in an archive.\n\nRemaining known responsibilities are the four open broader-adoption follow-ups, the expressly retained Packaging migration debt, and present proof gaps allocated by the approved complete-model-test-coverage plan. Those remain with their named owners. If current M6 wishes to rely on a historical result beyond its exact recorded scope, the responsible current assessor must disposition applicability or obtain new proof; this note does not waive it. No unsupported quantitative improvement or current universal conformance is established.\n\n## Exact inspected record identities\n\nEach row is the SHA-256 of current retained original bytes, with last-touch Git commit.     "8fbd4649412c68be1b06ef4479e86f01c9469e0a4109f3af5be866eaa2cd6d44": "{\n  \"finding\": {\n    \"id\": \"F1\",\n    \"reporter\": \"R\",\n    \"location\": \"tests/test_importer.py\",\n    \"evidence\": \"Original rejection assertion checked only error, not destination.\",\n    \"required_outcome\": \"Assert full preserved destination after invalid middle row.\",\n    \"resolution\": \"resolved by R after independent current test inspection\",\n    \"current_fields_editable\": true\n  },\n  \"blocker\": {\n    \"id\": \"B1\",\n    \"reporter\": \"V\",\n    \"origin\": {\n      \"subject\": \"original failing implementation Sbad\",\n      \"evidence\": \"T-invalid after was [[9,\\\"retained\\\"],[1,\\\"alpha\\\"]]\",\n      \"judgment\": \"failed Verify\"\n    },\n    \"disposition\": \"closed by V after direct proof and independent reassessment\"\n  }\n}\n",
    "1190d67e6944426605f367aa5760b81929e685c58c358c9b388929d4e573c672": "{\n  \"source\": \"Supplied item observations; no actual commands run in packet creation\",\n  \"items\": [\n    {\n      \"id\": \"atomic-import\",\n      \"command\": \"old-batch-check\",\n      \"outcome\": \"passed\",\n      \"subject_sha256\": \"b64fd1ab9eb77b30a1f4791534cacd0e65c5e0e517f02041f615f302cb5b5ba1\",\n      \"observed\": true,\n      \"freshness\": \"current\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"generated\",\n      \"observation\": \"Actual packet bytes match derived/import-contract.txt and dist/import-contract.txt\",\n      \"outcome\": \"passed\",\n      \"observed\": true,\n      \"freshness\": \"current\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"hosted_ci\": {\n    \"outcome\": \"not-run\",\n    \"applicable\": false,\n    \"reason\": \"Non-Git local assessment; no remote handoff requested.\"\n  }\n}\n",
    "58aadaf00c8e31473e2ac1af163a33d95cb771ae3f9397e83f2a1305d268a1c2": "{\n  \"kind\": \"synthetic authoritative decision snapshot, not a CLI envelope or stored v3 record\",\n  \"change_id\": \"fixture-atomic-import\",\n  \"record_contract\": \"rigorloop-records-v3\",\n  \"governed_recording_context\": true,\n  \"workflow_managed\": true,\n  \"current_milestone\": \"M2\",\n  \"milestone_owner\": \"implement\",\n  \"milestone_state\": \"in-progress\",\n  \"milestones\": {\n    \"M1\": \"completed\",\n    \"M2\": \"in-progress\"\n  },\n  \"authors\": [\n    \"A\"\n  ],\n  \"reviewer\": \"R\",\n  \"verifier\": \"V\",\n  \"independence\": \"R and V contributed no reviewed target bytes\",\n  \"approved_design_review\": \"DR-M6\",\n  \"approved_delivery_review\": \"DL-M6\",\n  \"design_members\": [\n    \"importer/importer.md\",\n    \"importer/examples/rejection.md\",\n    \"importer/test-design/cases.md\",\n    \"parent.md\"\n  ],\n  \"primary_plan\": \"plan.md\",\n  \"basis_scope\": \"exact current copied M4 full artifacts plus complete M6 implementation/proof fixtures\",\n  \"authorization\": \"Implement current M2 only; separate review and Verify; no external effects\",\n  \"applicability\": \"current\",\n  \"review_recording_receipt\": \"saved\",\n  \"activity_owner\": \"route\",\n  \"pending_owners\": [\n    \"implement\"\n  ]\n}\n",
    "29e3933e4f21da6ab01bd6d4e8865b02c2a8a91c6cc489636403f85bee5db93b": "{\n  \"kind\": \"synthetic authoritative decision snapshot, not a CLI envelope or stored v3 record\",\n  \"change_id\": \"fixture-atomic-import\",\n  \"record_contract\": null,\n  \"governed_recording_context\": false,\n  \"workflow_managed\": false,\n  \"current_milestone\": \"M2\",\n  \"milestone_owner\": \"implement\",\n  \"milestone_state\": \"in-progress\",\n  \"milestones\": {\n    \"M1\": \"completed\",\n    \"M2\": \"in-progress\"\n  },\n  \"authors\": [\n    \"A\"\n  ],\n  \"reviewer\": \"R\",\n  \"verifier\": \"V\",\n  \"independence\": \"R and V contributed no reviewed target bytes\",\n  \"approved_design_review\": \"DR-M6\",\n  \"approved_delivery_review\": \"DL-M6\",\n  \"design_members\": [\n    \"importer/importer.md\",\n    \"importer/examples/rejection.md\",\n    \"importer/test-design/cases.md\",\n    \"parent.md\"\n  ],\n  \"primary_plan\": \"plan.md\",\n  \"basis_scope\": \"exact current copied M4 full artifacts plus complete M6 implementation/proof fixtures\",\n  \"authorization\": \"Clear isolated import implementation with exact unchanged requirements and named proof; no planned/armed execution\",\n  \"applicability\": \"current\",\n  \"review_recording_receipt\": \"saved\",\n  \"activity_owner\": \"route\",\n  \"pending_owners\": [\n    \"implement\"\n  ]\n}\n",
    "2121aaa810408d4c01d193d3cdcdec5ecad0e07a21205dfb85f91338ebebc6a3": "def import_rows(rows, destination):\n    parsed = []\n    seen = set()\n    for item in rows:\n        identifier, value = item\n        if not isinstance(identifier, int) or not isinstance(value, str) or not value or identifier in seen:\n            raise ValueError(\"invalid row\")\n        seen.add(identifier)\n        parsed.append((identifier, value))\n        destination.append((identifier, value))\n    return len(parsed)\n",
    "65556316be89a247a5f65a7bc609c48f05146c910fb50a7aaec4d0af8d80362b": "import unittest\nfrom src.importer import import_rows\n\nclass ImportTests(unittest.TestCase):\n    def test_valid_batch(self):\n        destination = [(9, \"retained\")]\n        self.assertEqual(import_rows([(1,\"alpha\"),(2,\"beta\"),(3,\"gamma\")], destination), 3)\n        self.assertEqual(destination, [(9,\"retained\"),(1,\"alpha\"),(2,\"beta\"),(3,\"gamma\")])\n    def test_invalid_middle_preserves_destination(self):\n        destination = [(9, \"retained\")]\n        before = list(destination)\n        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, \"invalid row\"):\n            import_rows([(1,\"alpha\"),(2,\"\"),(3,\"gamma\")], destination)\n    def test_unlisted_space_value_regression(self):\n        destination = [(9,\"retained\")]\n        self.assertEqual(import_rows([(1,\"alpha beta\")], destination), 1)\n        self.assertEqual(destination, [(9,\"retained\"),(1,\"alpha beta\")])\n    def test_row_domain_rejects_without_mutation(self):\n        for rows in [[(\"one\",\"alpha\")],[(1,\"\")],[(1,\"alpha\"),(1,\"beta\")]]:\n            with self.subTest(rows=rows):\n                destination = [(9,\"retained\")]\n                before = list(destination)\n                with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,\"invalid row\"):\n                    import_rows(rows, destination)\n        ",
    "bab35fe9c0f9c116ce6514653596f1d3cf95d0a86c87c17f3cbca83f50d7ba02": "{\n  \"claim\": \"Supplied deterministic observations to inspect method; no actual command executed\",\n  \"steps\": [\n    {\n      \"order\": 1,\n      \"actor\": \"A\",\n      \"action\": \"Add full before/after assertion to test before modifying production source\",\n      \"source_sha256\": \"2121aaa810408d4c01d193d3cdcdec5ecad0e07a21205dfb85f91338ebebc6a3\",\n      \"test_sha256\": \"95ec9f16e1c2308b7ed58871d7a0b76f288c3d66b1110f42dad1d6ceb17a951b\",\n      \"command\": \"old-batch-check\",\n      \"result\": \"failed\",\n      \"specific_observation\": {\n        \"initial\": [\n          [\n            9,\n            \"retained\"\n          ]\n        ],\n        \"input\": [\n          [\n            1,\n            \"alpha\"\n          ],\n          [\n            2,\n            \"\"\n          ],\n          [\n            3,\n            \"gamma\"\n          ]\n        ],\n        \"expected_after\": [\n          [\n            9,\n            \"retained\"\n          ]\n        ],\n        \"observed_after\": [\n          [\n            9,\n            \"retained\"\n          ],\n          [\n            1,\n            \"alpha\"\n          ]\n        ]\n      }\n    },\n    {\n      \"order\": 2,\n      \"actor\": \"A\",\n      \"action\": \"Move one complete destination update after all validation\",\n      \"source_sha256\": \"b64fd1ab9eb77b30a1f4791534cacd0e65c5e0e517f02041f615f302cb5b5ba1\",\n      \"test_sha256\": \"95ec9f16e1c2308b7ed58871d7a0b76f288c3d66b1110f42dad1d6ceb17a951b\",\n      \"commands\": [\n        \"row-check\",\n        \"old-batch-check\"\n      ],\n      \"result\": \"passed\",\n      \"observations_file\": \"evidence/import-trace.json\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"environment\": \"fixture-python-v1; actual mutable local list destination, no network/service/outage guarantee\",\n  \"commit\": {\n    \"id\": \"cccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccccc\",\n    \"subject\": \"M2: Preserve destination on invalid import\",\n    \"body\": \"Validation evidence: row-check and old-batch-check supplied passed trace bound to exact code/test identities.\"\n  }\n}\n",
    "b0ffdc9b7a88f40046070bdf46edc493df7658f932eee5fb76a86742121bdf78": "# Implementation evidence\n\nScope: IMPORT-SR-01/02 on the exact approved M2.     "12eeda7c43303fb3cbdbd3a6b8103facf9d0d5439283ba33060e1cca7a1bc083": "# Importer Design\n\nModel validation contract: model-document-v1\n\n## Introduction and goals\nOwner importer combines child Input validation and child Store persistence. Preserve the approved invalid-batch no-write outcome, with one current owner for each child rule and this composed guarantee.\n\n## Architecture constraints\nDirection is the exact approved proposal; no remote publication is authorized. Input supplies validated rows; Store accepts the complete validated batch. Installation, public release and outage/concurrency guarantees are outside this bounded fixture.\n\n## Architecture overview\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n  User[\"Local caller\"] -->|batch request| Importer\n  subgraph ImporterModel[\"Importer model\"]\n    Importer[\"Batch orchestration\"] -->|validate complete batch| Input[\"Input child: row and duplicate rules\"]\n    Input -->|validated batch or rejection| Importer\n    Importer -->|only validated batch| Store\n    Store[\"Store child: validated batch persistence\"]\n  end\n  Store -->|apply validated rows| Destination[\"Existing local destination\"]\n  Importer -->|complete success or validation rejection| User\n```\nThe model owns the orchestration and its two child responsibilities; the caller and destination are external interfaces. Detailed owners are the [context](#context-view), [building blocks](#building-block-view) and [runtime](#runtime-and-interfaces), rather than this summary alone.\n\n| View | Necessity and reason | Owning detail |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Context | Necessary: local caller, accepted direction and destination have distinct external roles. | [Context view](#context-view) |\n| Building Block | Necessary: orchestration, input validation and persistence have different duties and their composition can fail. | [Building Block view](#building-block-view) |\n| Runtime | Necessary: rejection must precede any write. | [Runtime and interfaces](#runtime-and-interfaces) |\n| Deployment | Not necessary: one local process/private destination; no separately deployed service or environment-specific guarantee is selected. | Architecture constraints; adding concurrency or outage behavior requires reassessment. |\n\n## Context view\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n  Direction[\"Approved direction owner\"] -.->|invalid input must preserve prior records| ImporterSystem[\"Importer\"]\n  Caller[\"Local caller\"] -->|complete input batch| ImporterSystem\n  ImporterSystem -->|success or specific validation rejection| Caller\n  ImporterSystem -->|validated batch updates only| Destination[\"Existing local destination\"]\n```\nThe caller submits data and receives the actual result; it does not bypass validation. The direction owner defines the supported invalid-input guarantee and remains the recipient of material infeasibility. The local destination begins with independent prior records and may change only after complete validation. Network publication and outage/concurrency semantics are outside the selected contract.\n\n## Building Block view\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n  Batch[\"Public batch interface\"] -->|entire request| Orchestrator[\"Importer orchestration\"]\n  Orchestrator -->|all rows and IDs| Validator[\"Input child\"]\n  Validator -->|validated whole batch or defined rejection| Orchestrator\n  Orchestrator -->|one complete validated batch after success| Writer[\"Store child\"]\n  Writer -->|apply rows| LocalRecords[\"Local destination\"]\n  Orchestrator -->|actual completion or rejection| Result[\"Caller result\"]\n```\nInput owns integer-ID, nonempty-value and within-batch duplicate checks under IMPORT-SR-01. Store owns the stipulated local operation accepting one complete validated batch; it does not decide input acceptance or authorize partial imports. Importer owns the invocation order and caller result under IMPORT-SR-02 and IMPORT-DEC-01. Neither child's isolated proof establishes that the public importer waits for complete validation; that interaction needs the real destination observations in C0.\n\n## Requirements\n| ID | Required behavior |\n| --- | --- |\n| IMPORT-SR-01 | Each row has an integer ID and nonempty value; duplicate IDs within the batch reject. |\n| IMPORT-SR-02 | An invalid batch leaves the destination unchanged; a valid batch commits every row. |\n\n### Boundary scan and acceptance scenarios\n| Dimension | Requirement basis | Distinct outcome to demonstrate |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Input domain | IMPORT-SR-01 | Integer/nonempty/duplicate distinctions yield exact acceptance or rejection. |\n| State/lifecycle | IMPORT-SR-02 | Invalid input preserves original rows; valid batch adds all rows. |\n| Identity/authority | - | Not applicable: local input has no principal/authorization rule in this bounded model. |\n| Composition/path | IMPORT-SR-02 | Full validation precedes Store invocation; per-row validation alone cannot prove this. |\n| Temporal/retry | - | Not applicable: retries and concurrent writers are explicitly outside this fixture's supported scope. |\n| Failure/recovery | IMPORT-SR-01, IMPORT-SR-02 | Invalid middle row gives nonzero rejection and no destination mutation. |\n| Compatibility/migration | - | Not applicable: no persisted-format migration or retired operation is introduced. |\n| External/environment | IMPORT-SR-02 | Observe the real local destination; no network dependency is claimed. |\n\n## Runtime and interfaces\nThe importer validates all rows and duplicate IDs before invoking Store once. If the middle row is invalid, it returns a validation failure without invoking Store. The valid batch write adds all three rows to the existing destination. Children do not own the parent's sequencing guarantee.\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n  User->>Importer: batch\n  Importer->>Input: all rows\n  alt invalid row\n    Input-->>Importer: rejection\n    Importer-->>User: nonzero validation failure\n  else all valid\n    Input-->>Importer: validated complete batch\n    Importer->>Store: write complete batch\n    Importer-->>User: complete success\n  end\n```\n\n## Test design\nSee [owned coverage](test-design/cases.md). IMPORT-TC-01/02 retain conditions, independent observations and proposed realization here, independently of a Delivery plan. Parent owns the Input-to-Store no-write interaction; per-row checks cannot observe it.\n\n## Decisions and risk\nIMPORT-DEC-01 chooses complete validation before write over a validate/write loop: the latter can leave row1 behind after row2 fails. Preserve that alternative and consequence. Unknown protected behavior returns to Design; material infeasibility returns to the direction owner.\n\n## Supporting examples\n[Example E0](examples/rejection.md) is an explicitly declared illustration of IMPORT-SR-02, not a workflow record or executed test.\n\n## Limits and handoff\nExact model, coverage and relied-on example go to independent Design Review. Proposed tests are unexecuted. No authored document or save receipt supplies approval.\n\nUnresolved owner decision: partial-write outcome has not been selected for the requested new scope.\n",

    "6c3950437b479d9b77a9b32a489fb1109fb57e93833da65b45777351dae6813f": "def import_rows(rows, destination):\n    parsed = []\n    seen = set()\n    for item in rows:\n        identifier, value = item\n        if not isinstance(identifier, int) or not isinstance(value, str) or not value or identifier in seen:\n            raise ValueError(\"invalid row\")\n        seen.add(identifier)\n        parsed.append((identifier, value))\n    destination.extend(parsed)\n    return len(parsed)\n\n# Changed accepted-value policy under review.\n",

    "eb3a72322470a7dd3ef369d8910a9e5c19d9738fbdff9c530a1e50097c1e7004": "{\n  \"finding\": {\n    \"id\": \"F1\",\n    \"reporter\": \"R\",\n    \"location\": \"tests/test_importer.py\",\n    \"evidence\": \"Original rejection assertion checked only error, not destination.\",\n    \"required_outcome\": \"Assert full preserved destination after invalid middle row.\",\n    \"resolution\": \"open\",\n    \"current_fields_editable\": true\n  },\n  \"blocker\": {\n    \"id\": \"B1\",\n    \"reporter\": \"V\",\n    \"origin\": {\n      \"subject\": \"original failing implementation Sbad\",\n      \"evidence\": \"T-invalid after was [[9,\\\"retained\\\"],[1,\\\"alpha\\\"]]\",\n      \"judgment\": \"failed Verify\"\n    },\n    \"disposition\": \"open\"\n  }\n}\n",
    "674243f65cc83d80bbf6fcc91be8207924eb4c64b616afc896d4a070cb83bf23": "import unittest\nfrom src.importer import import_rows\n\nclass ImportTests(unittest.TestCase):\n    def test_valid_batch(self):\n        destination = [(9, \"retained\")]\n        self.assertEqual(import_rows([(1,\"alpha\"),(2,\"beta\"),(3,\"gamma\")], destination), 3)\n        self.assertEqual(destination, [(9,\"retained\"),(1,\"alpha\"),(2,\"beta\"),(3,\"gamma\")])\n    def test_invalid_middle_preserves_destination(self):\n        destination = [(9, \"retained\")]\n        before = list(destination)\n        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, \"invalid row\"):\n            import_rows([(1,\"alpha\"),(2,\"\"),(3,\"gamma\")], destination)\n    def test_unlisted_space_value_regression(self):\n        destination = [(9,\"retained\")]\n        self.assertEqual(import_rows([(1,\"alpha beta\")], destination), 1)\n        self.assertEqual(destination, [(9,\"retained\"),(1,\"alpha beta\")])\n",
    "52f77f9b1536e044fe821c8da773f8102fdc333d8e8d455e115f4d8f9cdc79d6": "{\n  \"finding\": {\n    \"id\": \"F1\",\n    \"reporter\": \"R\",\n    \"location\": \"tests/test_importer.py\",\n    \"evidence\": \"tests/test_importer.py omitted full destination equality after the expected ValueError.\",\n    \"required_outcome\": \"Assert full preserved destination after invalid middle row.\",\n    \"resolution\": \"open\",\n    \"current_fields_editable\": true\n  },\n  \"blocker\": {\n    \"id\": \"B1\",\n    \"reporter\": \"V\",\n    \"origin\": {\n      \"subject\": \"original failing implementation Sbad\",\n      \"evidence\": \"T-invalid after was [[9,\\\"retained\\\"],[1,\\\"alpha\\\"]]\",\n      \"judgment\": \"failed Verify\"\n    },\n    \"disposition\": \"open\"\n  }\n}\n",

    "d93e1cfd80b5ff436f8f48cf10d92b1f90c6ee4a2c2f76efeea8dbee3efd6dad": "# Batch import delivery plan\n\n## Purpose / big picture\n\nDeliver the approved local batch-import behavior without weakening invalid-batch destination preservation. This is stable synthetic allocation; no command, current review result or milestone completion is claimed.\n\n## Current Handoff Summary\n\n- Owning change record: docs/changes/batch-import/change.json (stable synthetic pointer supplied by authority.md; this packet contains no runtime record and claims no record-schema conformance)\n\nMutable lifecycle state, current milestone state, review status, blockers, routing, and closeout readiness live only in this record.\n\n## Source artifacts\n\n- Proposal: [Direction](proposal.md)\n- Spec: [Design D0](importer/importer.md); living model, no legacy feature output\n- Architecture: [Importer architecture](importer/importer.md#architecture-overview)\n- Prior-contract test spec: none; model-owned [C0 coverage](importer/test-design/cases.md) supplies intent, with no companion test-spec\n\n## Context and orientation\n\n[Design D0](importer/importer.md) and [coverage C0](importer/test-design/cases.md) own the behavior and observations. Importer combines Input validation and Store persistence; [(9, retained)] is the original private destination. D0/C0 assessments and command availability are expressly fictional supplied facts. [Authority](authority.md) bounds actions; history and neighbor.txt remain preserved.\n\n## Non-goals\n\n- No network, remote publication, actual command execution, automatic continuation, storage-outage/concurrency guarantee or change to single-row behavior.\n\n## Requirements covered\n\n- IMPORT-SR-01: M1 and TG-1 input decisions.\n- IMPORT-SR-02: M2 and TG-2 actual success/rejection destination observations; TG-FINAL-1 checks their composed path.\n- R-old: preserve the existing unusual-valid-value regression; catalog absence alone does not permit removal.\n\n## Milestones\n\n### M1. Input validation\n\n- Milestone kind: implementation\n- Engineering purpose: Establish the complete input decision before any persistence connection.\n- Requirements: IMPORT-SR-01\n- Architecture responsibility: Input child; importer owns later invocation order under IMPORT-DEC-01.\n- Dependencies:\n  - Current proposal and exact D0/C0 have the separately stipulated independent assessments; this allocation grants no implementation permission.\n- Implementation scope: Implement row and duplicate-ID checks only; retain existing single-row behavior and introduce no persistence writes.\n- Files/components likely touched:\n  - Input validation component and local partition tests.\n- Required verification:\n  - TG-1 — Integer IDs, nonempty values and duplicate IDs select the intended acceptance/rejection; valid rows remain accepted.\n- Evidence expectations: Literal valid/invalid rows, intended diagnostics, exact subject identities and actual outcomes when executed.\n- Implementation steps:\n  - Establish the valid three-row control, introduce each defining invalid condition in a fresh input, prove expected failure when feasible, implement the rule and rerun the same proof.\n- Validation commands:\n  - row-check (stipulated project-supported command label; no invocation is performed by this packet).\n- Expected observable result: All three valid rows pass; each malformed row or duplicate-ID batch produces its intended rejection.\n- Completion criteria: Every allocated input partition has independent expected observations and current passing proof; no intended behavior remains unresolved.\n- Required evidence: Input/source/test identities, actual row-check observations and limitations in implementation-owned evidence.\n- Review handoff: Independent milestone Code Review of the Input change and its concrete assertions before dependent connection work.\n- Optional commit boundary: `M1: Input validation`\n- Risks:\n  - A broken input fixture can masquerade as rejection protection.\n- Rollback/recovery:\n  - Restore the local Input slice if a valid control or protected partition fails; preserve unrelated user data and do not begin M2.\n\n### M2. Batch write boundary\n\n- Milestone kind: implementation\n- Engineering purpose: Connect complete validation to persistence as one reviewable change with its allocated proof.\n- Requirements: IMPORT-SR-02; preserve IMPORT-SR-01 and R-old.\n- Architecture responsibility: Importer orchestration of Input and Store, IMPORT-DEC-01, with parent-owned validation-to-write observation.\n- Dependencies:\n  - M1's current independent milestone review and complete input behavior.\n- Implementation scope: Connect the complete validator to one batch Store call; preserve single-row behavior. Outage/concurrency guarantees remain outside this fixture.\n- Files/components likely touched:\n  - Importer batch connection and its allocated test fixtures.\n- Required verification:\n  - TG-2 — Prove both IMPORT-TC-01/02 through the real public boundary and private destination with independent before/after rows.\n- Evidence expectations: Exact subjects, inputs, intended diagnostics and independent complete destination snapshots for success and rejection; preserve the unusual-valid-value R-old regression.\n- Implementation steps:\n  - First prove both IMPORT-TC-01/02 through the real public boundary and private destination with independent before/after rows. Then implement the connection, rerun required proof and retain the independent expected records.\n- Validation commands:\n  - row-check (stipulated command label; no invocation is performed by this packet).\n- Expected observable result: Valid input leaves exactly [(9, retained), (1, alpha), (2, beta), (3, gamma)]; invalid middle value reports that rejection and leaves exactly [(9, retained)].\n- Completion criteria: Current allocated observations pass and independent milestone review is complete; an unresolved required observation blocks completion.\n- Required evidence: Implementation-owned exact subjects, command, complete destination observations and limitations; no authored allocation is a passing run.\n- Review handoff: Independent Code Review of the complete batch connection and allocated assertions; preserve distinct final whole-change review.\n- Optional commit boundary: `M2: Batch write boundary`\n- Risks:\n  - A per-row write loop can commit row1 before rejecting row2; valid-only observations would miss it.\n- Rollback/recovery:\n  - Revert the connection and its fixtures together if required protection fails, preserving M1, prior records and unrelated work.\n\n\n## Final review checkpoint\n\n- Kind: lifecycle-closeout.\n- Dependency: all in-scope implementation milestones and required corrections complete.\n- Assessment: fresh independent final whole-change Code Review of the complete delivered engineering change and cross-milestone interactions.\n- Evidence: exact final subjects, independent reviewer basis, judgment and concern dispositions.\n- Successor: final Verify; corrections return to their owner and require affected reassessment.\n\nThis checkpoint applies the selected review policy. A verification-group non-applicability rationale does not waive it.\n\n## Change-level verification\n\n### TG-FINAL-1. Preserve complete batch behavior across Input and Store\n\n- Covers: IMPORT-SR-01/02, M1/M2, public import-to-Store path and retained R-old protection.\n- Demonstrate: Invoke the same public boundary on valid rows and a fresh invalid-middle-row variation. Compare the entire destination to independently specified rows; an implementation that leaves (1, alpha) after rejection must fail. Preserve the unusual valid value and expose actual diagnostics.\n- Evidence expectations: Actual project-supported batch proof with private real persistence, exact subjects/commands/inputs/results and independent Code Review; this packet supplies no execution results.\n- Non-applicability: Not claimed: the composed public path remains required even if local child checks pass. Required final whole-change Code Review is a separate checkpoint.\n\n## Validation plan\n\n- row-check: input partitions and local rejection meaning.\n- row-check: IMPORT-TC-01/02 on the real public/private-destination boundary with complete independent before/after rows, plus R-old.\n- Independent artifact/implementation review: inspect scope, exact subjects, sequencing and whether actual assertions detect partial persistence; no phrase check substitutes for judgment.\n- Command labels are stipulated fixture inputs, not repository commands to execute; confirm actual project support before implementation.\n\n## Risks and recovery\n\n- Risk: Per-row writes or a parser-only replacement can hide partial persistence. Mutable results embedded in this plan would create false authority.\n  - Recovery: Revert the affected connection and its fixtures together, preserve M1 and unrelated user records, and return missing behavior/allocation to the responsible owner. Keep observed results outside stable intent.\n\n## Dependencies\n\n- Current exact proposal/Design authority precedes Plan reliance; M1 proof/review precedes M2; all implementation/corrections precede fresh whole-change Code Review and distinct final Verify. No external action follows from this allocation.\n\n## Decision log\n\n| Date | Decision | Reason | Alternatives rejected |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| 2026-09-17 | Validate the complete batch before connecting one Store write; retain real destination proof after local input proof. | The approved invalid-input guarantee prohibits partial persistence, while dependency order keeps intermediate work reviewable. | Per-row validate/write can retain row1 after row2 fails; parser-only proof cannot detect that state. |\n\n\n## Readiness\n\n- See the owning change record for current workflow state.\n\n## Defining allocated proof change\n\nAll batch/destination proof is replaced by row-check, which validates individual row syntax only and never invokes import_rows or reads the destination. This is the sole current proof allocation for SR-02.\n",

    "36da04d6b2d5d5ecd4d51d07d90afdfc9dfdc543330399152431d2218697b1ab": "# Local batch import\n\n## Challenge\nManual row entry makes a valid batch cumbersome and a rejected batch must not leave a partially updated destination.\n\n## Goals\nCommit every valid row; if any row is invalid, retain exactly the original destination. Make rejection visible to the user.\n\n## Scope and non-goals\nLocal batch import only. No remote publication, automatic next-stage execution, network service or change to existing single-row behavior. A runtime storage outage and concurrent writers require their own specified behavior before any broader guarantee is claimed; this fixture selects validation rejection.\n\n## Governing principle\nA rejected batch must leave the user where they started.\n\n## Proposed direction\nValidate a complete batch before crossing the persistence boundary. Preserve the all-or-nothing outcome for invalid input; Design owns realization.\n\n## Feasibility\nThe supplied storage interface supports one validated batch write. This is a bounded fixture assumption, not live provider evidence. If that assumption fails, return the constraint and options to the direction owner without weakening the goal.\n\n## Decision requested\nApprove this direction for independently reviewed Design work only. This draft grants no Design approval, implementation, publication or merge authority.\n\nCurrent proposed exception: permit partial destination writes on invalid input without declaring a scope change.\n",
    "a9f4f5ccbe3a521cbfa2a7a84ae7d67161811ff34e7fe418430735e3844df883": "# Decision packet rc-proposal-scope-loss\n\n## Exact request\n\nReview the current full proposal against current Vision.\n\n## Authority, conditions and evidence\n\nHidden partial-write exception contradicts the preserved atomic-import goal.\n\n## Procedure and limits\n\nRead the full selected methods listed in method-inputs.json, then the complete before tree and ordered proposed action.     "6d75be373302c4c066b645cf657d5db05f941638f41a9f7bacdaf59ce91621a3": "# Local batch import\n\n## Challenge\nManual row entry makes a valid batch cumbersome and a rejected batch must not leave a partially updated destination.\n\n## Goals\nCommit every valid row; if any row is invalid, retain exactly the original destination. Make rejection visible to the user.\n\n## Scope and non-goals\nLocal batch import only. No remote publication, automatic next-stage execution, network service or change to existing single-row behavior. A runtime storage outage and concurrent writers require their own specified behavior before any broader guarantee is claimed; this fixture selects validation rejection.\n\n## Governing principle\nA rejected batch must leave the user where they started.\n\n## Proposed direction\nValidate a complete batch before crossing the persistence boundary. Preserve the all-or-nothing outcome for invalid input; Design owns realization.\n\n## Feasibility\nThe supplied storage interface supports one validated batch write. This is a bounded fixture assumption, not live provider evidence. If that assumption fails, return the constraint and options to the direction owner without weakening the goal.\n\n## Decision requested\nApprove this direction for independently reviewed Design work only. This draft grants no Design approval, implementation, publication or merge authority.\n\nSelected direction now commits valid prefixes of invalid batches; owner has not accepted a vision exception.\n",

    "79d541a1484b55f75a8f9f748b8b6abba123c46ad942ff1b6a54f6588c4bf729": "def import_rows(rows, destination):\n    parsed = []\n    seen = set()\n    for item in rows:\n        identifier, value = item\n        if not isinstance(identifier, int) or not isinstance(value, str) or not value or identifier in seen:\n            raise ValueError(\"invalid row\")\n        seen.add(identifier)\n        parsed.append((identifier, value))\n    destination.extend(parsed)\n    return len(parsed)\n\n# New decision-bearing requested behavior: reject whitespace-only values.\n",

    "bfc933a25790a8028809f7aec4b40eb5a08bd751e6ce5d3ea09672a57c776e40": "import unittest\nfrom src.importer import import_rows\n\nclass ImportTests(unittest.TestCase):\n    def test_valid_batch(self):\n        destination = [(9, \"retained\")]\n        self.assertEqual(import_rows([(1,\"alpha\"),(2,\"beta\"),(3,\"gamma\")], destination), 3)\n        self.assertEqual(destination, [(9,\"retained\"),(1,\"alpha\"),(2,\"beta\"),(3,\"gamma\")])\n    def test_invalid_middle_preserves_destination(self):\n        destination = [(9, \"retained\")]\n        before = list(destination)\n        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, \"invalid row\"):\n            import_rows([(1,\"alpha\"),(2,\"\"),(3,\"gamma\")], destination)\n        self.assertEqual(destination, before)\n",

Fictional observations are inputs whose sufficiency is under assessment.\n- Open blockers: none\n- Final judgment: branch-ready within supplied basis\n- Next owner: isolated stop\n\n# Final verification explanation\n\n## Change and rationale\n\nAtomic import validates every row before a single destination update, preserving destination contents on invalid input while accepting the complete valid batch. The filled plan allocates both requirements and distinct final review.\n\n## Exact basis and evidence\n\nThe exact copied M4 Design/Plan and M6 implementation, fixtures and evidence are bound by subjects.json.     "6a50f6ecbd1e5f5bc5502d7cbfd9ca8b4872fbea54d14b5fb46fdcafc833d0ff": "## Purpose / big picture\n\nImplement the approved local atomic-import contract in one reviewable slice, with proof that invalid middle rows preserve the complete existing destination.\n\n## Current Handoff Summary\n\n- Owning change record: docs/changes/fixture-one-slice/change.json\n\nMutable lifecycle state, current milestone state, review status, blockers, routing, and closeout readiness live only in this record.\n\n## Source artifacts\n\n- Proposal: proposal.md\n- Spec: importer/importer.md\n- Architecture: importer/importer.md#building-block-view\n- Prior-contract test spec: none\n\n## Context and orientation\n\nThe importer validates integer IDs, nonempty values and within-batch uniqueness before one destination update. The existing destination is [(9,retained)]. Caller, validation and Store duties are distinct in the approved model. The code and tests are src/importer.py and tests/test_importer.py.\n\n## Non-goals\n\n- No external publication, concurrency protocol or storage-failure rollback promise.\n\n## Requirements covered\n\n- IMPORT-SR-01: M1 input partitions and row-check.\n- IMPORT-SR-02: M1 full-batch action and full destination observation; TG-FINAL-1.\n\n## Milestones\n\n### M1. Implement complete validation before one destination update\n\n- Milestone kind: implementation\n- Engineering purpose: Keep one coherent validation-to-destination boundary reviewable with its real acceptance and rejection observations.\n- Requirements: IMPORT-SR-01, IMPORT-SR-02\n- Architecture responsibility: Importer orchestration, Input row rules, Store update; IMPORT-DEC-01.\n- Dependencies:\n  - Current approved exact Design and Delivery Review before implementation.\n- Implementation scope: Update importer and its complete fixture/assertion set; upstream artifacts remain read-only.\n- Files/components likely touched:\n  - src/importer.py; tests/test_importer.py; implementation evidence.\n- Required verification:\n  - TG-1 — Valid three-row batch commits fully; invalid middle/ID/duplicate input rejects and preserves [(9,retained)]; retain R-old embedded-space acceptance.\n- Evidence expectations: Exact fresh fixture, public import action, error/result and full before/after rows with source/test identity.\n- Implementation steps:\n  - Add the failing full-destination proof before changing per-row writes. Validate the entire batch, write once, then run named proof and inspect unchanged surfaces.\n- Validation commands:\n  - row-check; old-batch-check (opaque supported fixture labels, not commands to run in the real repository).\n- Expected observable result: Every valid row appears after existing rows; every invalid input leaves exactly the original rows.\n- Completion criteria: All specified input partitions and composed outcomes protected, direct proof current, no known required fixture gap.\n- Required evidence: Stage-owned before/after proof, exact changed/unaffected surfaces, rollback unit and review handoff.\n- Review handoff: Independent Code Review of complete M1 code/test/evidence; implementation does not close its milestone.\n- Optional commit boundary: `M1: Preserve destination on invalid import`\n- Risks:\n  - Per-row mutation could leave a valid prefix after a later invalid row.\n- Rollback/recovery:\n  - Revert importer/test slice together, preserve prior destination/neighbor files, return missing behavior or allocation to its owner.\n\n## Final review checkpoint\n\n- Kind: lifecycle-closeout.\n- Dependency: all in-scope implementation milestones and required corrections complete.\n- Assessment: fresh independent final whole-change Code Review of the complete delivered engineering change and cross-milestone interactions.\n- Evidence: exact final subjects, independent reviewer basis, judgment and concern dispositions.\n- Successor: final Verify; corrections return to their owner and require affected reassessment.\n\nThis checkpoint applies the selected review policy. A verification-group non-applicability rationale does not waive it.\n\n## Change-level verification\n\n### TG-FINAL-1. Preserve Input-to-Store composition\n\n- Covers: IMPORT-SR-01/02, M1, public import and existing destination.\n- Demonstrate: Valid full commit and invalid no-write behavior on fresh fixtures, including R-old.\n- Evidence expectations: Actual selected project command observations with full independently expected destination and exact current subject; independent final review remains separate.\n- Non-applicability: Not claimed; local row tests alone cannot prove composition.\n\n## Validation plan\n\n- row-check: actual row domain partitions.\n- old-batch-check: public batch invocation and full destination observation.\n- Manual independent assessment: full artifact/code/test consistency and required final review before Verify.\n\n## Risks and recovery\n\n- Risk: A parser-only green check can conceal partial destination writes.\n  - Recovery: Repair the destination observation and return any upstream gap to its owner before closeout.\n\n## Dependencies\n\n- Exact upstream approvals, M1 implementation/review, fresh final whole-change review, then distinct Verify.\n\n## Decision log\n\n| Date | Decision | Reason | Alternatives rejected |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| 2026-09-18 | Validate complete batch before one Store update. | Invalid input must preserve all existing rows. | Per-row write can retain row1 after row2 fails. |\n\n## Readiness\n\n- See the owning change record for current workflow state.\n",

compare exact output bytes to input.upper().\",\n    \"check\": \"Full output bytes independently equal the declared transformation of exact input.\"\n  },\n  \"permission\": \"no install, publish or release authority\"\n}\n",
    "f7840559af5523f1f32b133bb028898b8f9cbb0185e956b718b0e188b5dc9d64": "# Decision packet skl-package-declared-transform\n\n## Exact request\n\nAssess the precise identity claim in the Packaging report.\n\n## Authority, conditions and evidence\n\nThe four actual private copies and report are present; source-to-product mechanisms remain with Packaging/Installation.
